Visitors use the guest Wi-Fi network, and the current password is on the screen at the reception desk.\\n\\n## Getting here\\n\\n-\"}, {\"doc_id\": \"oncall-incident-process\", \"title\": \"On-Call and Incident Process\", \"snippet\": \"laptop with a working connection within 15 minutes. Keep your phone charged and PagerLine notifications on. Do not be on call while traveling by air or while impaired. If you become unable to respond, hand the pager to the secondary and tell the rotation channel.\\n\\nThe secondary is the backup. The secondary responds if the primary does not acknowledge, and joins any SEV1 incident on the rotation's systems.\\n\\n## 6. Severity levels\\n\\nThe first responder sets the severity. Rotations\\n\\nFive teams run on-call rotations.\\n\\n| Rotation | Covers | Hours |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| SRE | Cloud infrastructure, ingestion pipeline, databases | 24\"}, {\"doc_id\": \"gridlens-product-faq\", \"title\": \"GridLens Product FAQ\", \"snippet\": \"report?**\\nThe Meridian M4 reports on a schedule that can be configured for each customer. The factory default is defined in the firmware specification. Outage and power quality events are sent immediately and do not wait for the schedule.\\n\\n**How long is data kept?**\\nRaw readings are kept for 13 months. Rollups at 15 minutes are kept for 7 years. A customer can export its data at any time.\\n\\n**Where is data stored?**\\nData for customers in North America is stored in the United States. Data for customers in the European Union is stored in Frankfurt and does not leave the EU.\\n\\n**Who owns the\"}, {\"doc_id\": \"architecture-overview\", \"title\": \"GridLens Architecture Overview\", \"snippet\": \"report at once.\\n\\nThis overview follows a reading from the device to the screen. It names each service and says which team owns it. It ends with the operational facts that engineers most often need: regions, recovery targets, retention, and how we deploy.\\n\\n## 2. Two ways in\\n\\nData enters GridLens by one of two paths.\\n\\n**The device path.** Cardinal devices, the Meridian M4 and through it the Pulse S2, connect to the device broker over MQTT with TLS. The protocol is defined in the EmberOS 3 Firmware Specification. The broker is a cluster of six nodes per region. If possible we can move WACOG Dsk2Dsk report before next weekend. I am giving top priority to your request. I appreciate your patience.\\nthanks\\nkrishna \\n\\n -----Original Message-----\\nFrom: \\tSeverson, Russ  \\nSent:\\tFriday, August 24, 2001 8:43 AM\\nTo:\\tLattupally, Krishna\\nCc:\\tLim, Francis S.\\nSubject:\\tRE: WACOG Desk to Desk Report\\n\\nYou might want to check with Narasim on this, he was working in the past on\"}, {\"id\": \"em-04554\", \"subject\": \"Thank you for Scheduling\", \"date\": \"2001-06-07T18:10:08Z\", \"preview\": \"Hello Vince,\\n \\nI just wanted to confirm that I have you scheduled for a one-on-one training session on Friday, June 8th at 9:00 AM.  Please bring your laptop to EB2249B at your assigned time.\\n \\nThe process will take approximately an hour and we will go over making sure that your dial-up software works and that you understand the process.  If you are an EBS employee, be sure to bring your secure id token, otherwise, all you will need to bring is your laptop (we provide power supplies for Compaq and IBM laptops.)\\n \\nIf you need to cancel, or you are running late, please page me at 713-327-6618 an\"}, {\"id\": \"em-23088\", \"subject\": \"RE: West Desk\", \"date\": \"2001-08-21T15:48:56Z\", \"preview\": \"A lucent phone will be fine. I will only be on the desk for about 7-8 days so don't go to too much trouble. Do you have Keith and Frank's flight info for their trip to Calgary on Sept. 7 ?
